{\footnotesize\ttfamily  None sphinx.\+writers.\+latex.\+La\+Te\+XTranslator.\+\_\+visit\+\_\+sig\+\_\+parameter\+\_\+list (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{}]{self,  }\item[{\mbox{\hyperlink{classdocutils_1_1nodes_1_1Element}{Element}}}]{node,  }\item[{type\mbox{[}\mbox{\hyperlink{classdocutils_1_1nodes_1_1Element}{Element}}\mbox{]}     }]{parameter\+\_\+group }\end{DoxyParamCaption})\hspace{0.3cm}{\ttfamily [protected]}}

\begin{DoxyVerb}Visit a signature parameters or type parameters list.

The *parameter_group* value is the type of a child node acting as a required parameter
or as a set of contiguous optional parameters.

The caller is responsible for closing adding surrounding LaTeX macro argument start
